K-sample flow-matching generation with sequential denoising\\\\n  3. LIBERO data preprocessing (image, language, state → PI05 inputs)\\\\n\\\\nPreprocessing follows the official lerobot PI05 pipeline:\\\\n  - Images: resize_with_pad to 224×224 → float [0,1]\\\\n  - State: MEAN_STD normalize → discretize 256 bins → embed in text prompt\\\\n  - Action output: MEAN_STD unnormalize with mean/std stats\\\\n\\\\nKey differences from SmolVLA wrapper:\\\\n  - VLM backbone: PaliGemma 2B (hidden_size=2048) vs Idefics3 (960D)\\\\n  - State: embedded in text prompt (not as separate tensor)\\\\n  - Normalization: MEAN_STD\\\\n  - KV cache: HuggingFace DynamicCache (deep-copied per denoise step internally)\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport copy\\\\nimport math\\\\nfrom collections import deque\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\\\\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import (\\\\n    PI05Policy,\\\\n    make_att_2d_masks,\\\\n    resize_with_pad_torch,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\ntry:\\\\n    from utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\\\nexcept ImportError:\\\\n    from .utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\\\n\\\\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef split_action(action_7d):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Split LIBERO 7D action into 6D pose and 1D gripper.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return action_7d[..., :CORRECTION_DIM], action_7d[..., CORRECTION_DIM:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass Pi05Wrapper:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Frozen π0.5 for obs_features and K-sample generation.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE = 2048\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, pretrained_path: str = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\\\\\",\\\\n                 device: str = \\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\\\n        # Open-loop action chunk queue. PI05 emits chunk_size=50 actions per\\\\n        # inference call; replaying them open-loop (1 inference per 50 env\\\\n        # steps) is the official lerobot contract. Re-running inference each\\\\n        # env step and only consuming action[0] starves multi-step behaviors\\\\n        # like the gripper-close at chunk step ~20, which produces 0% SR.\\\\n        self._action_queue = deque()\\\\n        self.policy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, strict=False)\\\\n        self._fix_vision_tower_keys(pretrained_path)\\\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\\\n        self.policy.eval()\\\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\\\n\\\\n        self.model = self.policy.model  # PI05Pytorch\\\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE\\\\n\\\\n        # Tokenizer (PaliGemma / Gemma — same SentencePiece vocab)\\\\n        from transformers import AutoTokenizer\\\\n        _tok_candidates = [\\\\n            \\\\\\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"unsloth/gemma-2b\\\\\\\",\\\\n        ]\\\\n        self.tokenizer = None\\\\n        for _name in _tok_candidates:\\\\n            try:\\\\n                self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\\\n                    _name, local_files_only=(_name == _tok_candidates[0])\\\\n                )\\\\n                break\\\\n            except Exception:\\\\n                continue\\\\n        if self.tokenizer is None:\\\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\\\\\"Cannot load tokenizer from any of {_tok_candidates}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.tokenizer.padding_side = \\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\\\n\\\\n        # Load normalizer stats (MEAN_STD)\\\\n        model_dir = Path(pretrained_path)\\\\n        self._load_normalizer_stats(model_dir)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    def _fix_vision_tower_keys(self, pretrained_path: str):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Fix vision tower key mismatch between saved weights and model architecture.\\\\n        \\\\n        Saved weights use: .vision_tower.vision_model.X\\\\n        Model expects:     .vision_tower.X  (transformers >= 5.x)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        sd = load_file(str(Path(pretrained_path) / \\\\\\\"model.safetensors\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        remap = {}\\\\n        for k, v in sd.items():\\\\n            if \\\\\\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\\\\\" in k:\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 8474 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n      obs_features: (1, 2048)\\\\n            samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples = self.generate_k_samples(kv_cache, pad_masks, K=K)\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def generate_adaptive_k_samples(self, past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks,\\\\n                                     threshold, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Adaptive K: generate k_init samples, expand to k_full if L2 disagreement >= threshold.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        init_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_init\\\\n        )\\\\n        B = init_samples.shape[0]\\\\n\\\\n        if k_init == 2:\\\\n            diff = init_samples[:, 0, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, 1, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n            per_step_l2 = diff.norm(dim=-1)\\\\n            mean_l2 = per_step_l2.mean(dim=-1)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            dists = []\\\\n            for i in range(k_init):\\\\n                for j in range(i + 1, k_init):\\\\n                    diff = init_samples[:, i, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, j, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n                    dists.append(diff.norm(dim=-1).mean(dim=-1))\\\\n            mean_l2 = torch.stack(dists).mean(dim=0)\\\\n\\\\n        needs_full = (mean_l2 >= threshold).any().item()\\\\n        l2_val = mean_l2.item() if B == 1 else mean_l2.cpu().tolist()\\\\n\\\\n        if not needs_full:\\\\n            return init_samples, {\\\\n                'path': 'fast', 'actual_k': k_init, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n            }\\\\n\\\\n        extra_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_full - k_init\\\\n        )\\\\n        all_samples = torch.cat([init_samples, extra_samples], dim=1)\\\\n        return all_samples, {\\\\n            'path': 'full', 'actual_k': k_full, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples_adaptive(self, agentview_rgb, task_name,\\\\n                                               robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb=None,\\\\n                                               threshold=0.1, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"End-to-end adaptive K: obs → features + adaptive samples + metadata.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples, metadata = self.generate_adaptive_k_samples(\\\\n            kv_cache, pad_masks, threshold, k_init, k_full\\\\n        )\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples, metadata\\\\n\\\\n    def reset_action_queue(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Clear cached chunk. Call at the start of every rollout (env.reset).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self._action_queue.clear()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action(self, agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state,\\\\n                       eye_in_hand_rgb=None, K: int = 1):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Return one (FULL_ACTION_DIM,) numpy action per call, replaying the\\\\n        cached chunk before re-inferring. When the queue is empty, runs one\\\\n        PI05 inference to obtain a chunk_size-step chunk (mean over K samples\\\\n        if K>1) and pushes every step into the queue.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not self._action_queue:\\\\n            _obs_feat, samples = self.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n                agentview_rgb=agentview_rgb,\\\\n                task_name=task_name,\\\\n                robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n                eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand_rgb,\\\\n                K=K,\\\\n            )\\\\n            # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM) -- average over K\\\\n            chunk = samples[0].mean(dim=0)\\\\n            chunk_np = chunk.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n            for i in range(chunk_np.shape[0]):\\\\n                self._action_queue.append(chunk_np[i])\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.99}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"12460971-c2b7-496e-bf65-b1cba93fe402","uuid":"3d4e0737-0c54-479a-a664-ab2f6b31a7a3","timestamp":"2026-05-21T06:39:12.859Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nPi05 (π0.5) wrapper for ACR-VLA.\\\\n\\\\nProvides:\\\\n  1. obs_features extraction (mean-pooled VLM hidden states, 2048D)\\\\n  2. PI05Pytorch\\\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE\\\\n\\\\n        # Tokenizer (PaliGemma / Gemma — same SentencePiece vocab)\\\\n        from transformers import AutoTokenizer\\\\n        _tok_candidates = [\\\\n            \\\\\\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"unsloth/gemma-2b\\\\\\\",\\\\n        ]\\\\n        self.tokenizer = None\\\\n        for _name in _tok_candidates:\\\\n            try:\\\\n                self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\\\n                    _name, local_files_only=(_name == _tok_candidates[0])\\\\n                )\\\\n                break\\\\n            except Exception:\\\\n                continue\\\\n        if self.tokenizer is None:\\\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\\\\\"Cannot load tokenizer from any of {_tok_candidates}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.tokenizer.padding_side = \\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\\\n\\\\n        # Load normalizer stats (MEAN_STD)\\\\n        model_dir = Path(pretrained_path)\\\\n        self._load_normalizer_stats(model_dir)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    def _fix_vision_tower_keys(self, pretrained_path: str):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Fix vision tower key mismatch between saved weights and model architecture.\\\\n        \\\\n        Saved weights use: .vision_tower.vision_model.X\\\\n        Model expects:     .vision_tower.X  (transformers >= 5.x)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        sd = load_file(str(Path(pretrained_path) / \\\\\\\"model.safetensors\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        remap = {}\\\\n        for k, v in sd.items():\\\\n            if \\\\\\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\\\\\" in k:\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 8474 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n      obs_features: (1, 2048)\\\\n            samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples = self.generate_k_samples(kv_cache, pad_masks, K=K)\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def generate_adaptive_k_samples(self, past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks,\\\\n                                     threshold, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Adaptive K: generate k_init samples, expand to k_full if L2 disagreement >= threshold.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        init_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_init\\\\n        )\\\\n        B = init_samples.shape[0]\\\\n\\\\n        if k_init == 2:\\\\n            diff = init_samples[:, 0, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, j, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n                    dists.append(diff.norm(dim=-1).mean(dim=-1))\\\\n            mean_l2 = torch.stack(dists).mean(dim=0)\\\\n\\\\n        needs_full = (mean_l2 >= threshold).any().item()\\\\n        l2_val = mean_l2.item() if B == 1 else mean_l2.cpu().tolist()\\\\n\\\\n        if not needs_full:\\\\n            return init_samples, {\\\\n                'path': 'fast', 'actual_k': k_init, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n            }\\\\n\\\\n        extra_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_full - k_init\\\\n        )\\\\n        all_samples = torch.cat([init_samples, extra_samples], dim=1)\\\\n        return all_samples, {\\\\n            'path': 'full', 'actual_k': k_full, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples_adaptive(self, agentview_rgb, task_name,\\\\n                                               robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb=None,\\\\n                                               threshold=0.1, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"End-to-end adaptive K: obs → features + adaptive samples + metadata.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples, metadata = self.generate_adaptive_k_samples(\\\\n            kv_cache, pad_masks, threshold, k_init, k_full\\\\n        )\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples, metadata\\\\n\\\\n    def reset_action_queue(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Clear cached chunk. When the queue is empty, runs one\\\\n        PI05 inference to obtain a chunk_size-step chunk (mean over K samples\\\\n        if K>1) and pushes every step into the queue.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not self._action_queue:\\\\n            _obs_feat, samples = self.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n                agentview_rgb=agentview_rgb,\\\\n                task_name=task_name,\\\\n                robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n                eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand_rgb,\\\\n                K=K,\\\\n            )\\\\n            # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM) -- average over K\\\\n            chunk = samples[0].mean(dim=0)\\\\n            chunk_np = chunk.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n            for i in range(chunk_np.shape[0]):\\\\n                self._action_queue.append(chunk_np[i])\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.99}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nPi05 (π0.5) wrapper for ACR-VLA.\\n\\nProvides:\\n  1. obs_features extraction (mean-pooled VLM hidden states, 2048D)\\n  2. K-sample flow-matching generation with sequential denoising\\n  3. LIBERO data preprocessing (image, language, state → PI05 inputs)\\n\\nPreprocessing follows the official lerobot PI05 pipeline:\\n  - Images: resize_with_pad to 224×224 → float [0,1]\\n  - State: MEAN_STD normalize → discretize 256 bins → embed in text prompt\\n  - Action output: MEAN_STD unnormalize with mean/std stats\\n\\nKey differences from SmolVLA wrapper:\\n  - VLM backbone: PaliGemma 2B (hidden_size=2048) vs Idefics3 (960D)\\n  - State: embedded in text prompt (not as separate tensor)\\n  - Normalization: MEAN_STD\\n  - KV cache: HuggingFace DynamicCache (deep-copied per denoise step internally)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\nfrom collections import deque\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\\n\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import (\\n    PI05Policy,\\n    make_att_2d_masks,\\n    resize_with_pad_torch,\\n)\\n\\ntry:\\n    from utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    from .utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\n\\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\\n\\n\\ndef split_action(action_7d):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Split LIBERO 7D action into 6D pose and 1D gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return action_7d[..., :CORRECTION_DIM], action_7d[..., CORRECTION_DIM:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n\\n\\nclass Pi05Wrapper:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Frozen π0.5 for obs_features and K-sample generation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE = 2048\\n\\n    def __init__(self, pretrained_path: str = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\",\\n                 device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\n        # Open-loop action chunk queue. PI05 emits chunk_size=50 actions per\\n        # inference call; replaying them open-loop (1 inference per 50 env\\n        # steps) is the official lerobot contract. Re-running inference each\\n        # env step and only consuming action[0] starves multi-step behaviors\\n        # like the gripper-close at chunk step ~20, which produces 0% SR.\\n        self._action_queue = deque()\\n        self.policy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, strict=False)\\n        self._fix_vision_tower_keys(pretrained_path)\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\n        self.policy.eval()\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        self.model = self.policy.model  # PI05Pytorch\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE\\n\\n        # Tokenizer (PaliGemma / Gemma — same SentencePiece vocab)\\n        from transformers import AutoTokenizer\\n        _tok_candidates = [\\n            \\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\",\\n            \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer\\\",\\n            \\\"unsloth/gemma-2b\\\",\\n        ]\\n        self.tokenizer = None\\n        for _name in _tok_candidates:\\n            try:\\n                self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n                    _name, local_files_only=(_name == _tok_candidates[0])\\n                )\\n                break\\n            except Exception:\\n                continue\\n        if self.tokenizer is None:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Cannot load tokenizer from any of {_tok_candidates}\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer.padding_side = \\\"right\\\"\\n\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\n\\n        # Load normalizer stats (MEAN_STD)\\n        model_dir = Path(pretrained_path)\\n        self._load_normalizer_stats(model_dir)\\n\\n\\n    def _fix_vision_tower_keys(self, pretrained_path: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Fix vision tower key mismatch between saved weights and model architecture.\\n        \\n        Saved weights use: .vision_tower.vision_model.X\\n        Model expects:     .vision_tower.X  (transformers >= 5.x)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        sd = load_file(str(Path(pretrained_path) / \\\"model.safetensors\\\"))\\n        remap = {}\\n        for k, v in sd.items():\\n            if \\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\" in k:\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 8474 字符] ...\\n\\n      obs_features: (1, 2048)\\n            samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\n        )\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\n        )\\n        samples = self.generate_k_samples(kv_cache, pad_masks, K=K)\\n        return obs_feat, samples\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def generate_adaptive_k_samples(self, past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks,\\n                                     threshold, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Adaptive K: generate k_init samples, expand to k_full if L2 disagreement >= threshold.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        init_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_init\\n        )\\n        B = init_samples.shape[0]\\n\\n        if k_init == 2:\\n            diff = init_samples[:, 0, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, 1, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n            per_step_l2 = diff.norm(dim=-1)\\n            mean_l2 = per_step_l2.mean(dim=-1)\\n        else:\\n            dists = []\\n            for i in range(k_init):\\n                for j in range(i + 1, k_init):\\n                    diff = init_samples[:, i, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, j, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n                    dists.append(diff.norm(dim=-1).mean(dim=-1))\\n            mean_l2 = torch.stack(dists).mean(dim=0)\\n\\n        needs_full = (mean_l2 >= threshold).any().item()\\n        l2_val = mean_l2.item() if B == 1 else mean_l2.cpu().tolist()\\n\\n        if not needs_full:\\n            return init_samples, {\\n                'path': 'fast', 'actual_k': k_init, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\n            }\\n\\n        extra_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_full - k_init\\n        )\\n        all_samples = torch.cat([init_samples, extra_samples], dim=1)\\n        return all_samples, {\\n            'path': 'full', 'actual_k': k_full, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\n        }\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples_adaptive(self, agentview_rgb, task_name,\\n                                               robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb=None,\\n                                               threshold=0.1, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"End-to-end adaptive K: obs → features + adaptive samples + metadata.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\n        )\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\n        )\\n        samples, metadata = self.generate_adaptive_k_samples(\\n            kv_cache, pad_masks, threshold, k_init, k_full\\n        )\\n        return obs_feat, samples, metadata\\n\\n    def reset_action_queue(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Clear cached chunk. Call at the start of every rollout (env.reset).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self._action_queue.clear()\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def predict_action(self, agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state,\\n                       eye_in_hand_rgb=None, K: int = 1):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Return one (FULL_ACTION_DIM,) numpy action per call, replaying the\\n        cached chunk before re-inferring. When the queue is empty, runs one\\n        PI05 inference to obtain a chunk_size-step chunk (mean over K samples\\n        if K>1) and pushes every step into the queue.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not self._action_queue:\\n            _obs_feat, samples = self.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n                agentview_rgb=agentview_rgb,\\n                task_name=task_name,\\n                robot_state=robot_state,\\n                eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand_rgb,\\n                K=K,\\n            )\\n            # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM) -- average over K\\n            chunk = samples[0].mean(dim=0)\\n            chunk_np = chunk.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n            for i in range(chunk_np.shape[0]):\\n                self._action_queue.append(chunk_np[i])\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.99}}"}}}

Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\\\n\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Initialize the core PI05 model\\\\n        self.init_rtc_processor()\\\\n        self.model = PI05Pytorch(config, rtc_processor=self.rtc_processor)\\\\n\\\\n        # Enable gradient checkpointing if requested\\\\n        if config.gradient_checkpointing:\\\\n            self.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()\\\\n\\\\n        self.model.to(config.device)\\\\n\\\\n        self.reset()\\\\n\\\\n    @classmethod\\\\n    def from_pretrained(\\\\n        cls: builtins.type[T],\\\\n        pretrained_name_or_path: str | Path,\\\\n        *,\\\\n        config: PreTrainedConfig | None = None,\\\\n        force_download: bool = False,\\\\n        resume_download: bool | None = None,\\\\n        proxies: dict | None = None,\\\\n        token: str | bool | None = None,\\\\n        cache_dir: str | Path | None = None,\\\\n        local_files_only: bool = False,\\\\n        revision: str | None = None,\\\\n        strict: bool = True,\\\\n        **kwargs,\\\\n    ) -> T:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Override the from_pretrained method to handle key remapping and display important disclaimer.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        print(\\\\n            \\\\\\\"The PI05 model is a direct port of the OpenPI implementation. \\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n            \\\\\\\"This implementation follows the original OpenPI structure for compatibility. \\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n            \\\\\\\"Original implementation: https://github.com/Physical-Intelligence/openpi\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        if pretrained_name_or_path is None:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"pretrained_name_or_path is required\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        # Use provided config if available, otherwise create default config\\\\n        if config is None:\\\\n            config = PreTrainedConfig.from_pretrained(\\\\n                pretrained_name_or_path=pretrained_name_or_path,\\\\n                force_download=force_download,\\\\n                resume_download=resume_download,\\\\n                proxies=proxies,\\\\n                token=token,\\\\n                cache_dir=cache_dir,\\\\n                local_files_only=local_files_only,\\\\n                revision=revision,\\\\n                **kwargs,\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n        # Initialize model without loading weights\\\\n        # Check if dataset_stats were provided in kwargs\\\\n        model = cls(config, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Load state dict (expects keys with \\\\\\\"model.\\\\\\\" prefix)\\\\n        try:\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"Loading model from: {pretrained_name_or_path}\\\\\\\")\\\\n            try:\\\\n                from transformers.utils import cached_file\\\\n\\\\n                resolved_file = cached_file(\\\\n                    pretrained_name_or_path,\\\\n                    \\\\\\\"model.safetensors\\\\\\\",\\\\n                    cache_dir=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"cache_dir\\\\\\\"),\\\\n                    force_download=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"force_download\\\\\\\", False),\\\\n                    resume_download=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"resume_download\\\\\\\"),\\\\n                    proxies=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"proxies\\\\\\\"),\\\\n                    token=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"token\\\\\\\"),\\\\n                    revision=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"revision\\\\\\\"),\\\\n                    local_files_only=kwargs.get(\\\\\\\"local_files_only\\\\\\\", False),\\\\n                )\\\\n                from safetensors.torch import load_file\\\\n\\\\n                original_state_dict = load_file(resolved_file)\\\\n                print(\\\\\\\"✓ Loaded state dict from model.safetensors\\\\\\\")\\\\n            except Exception as e:\\\\n                print(f\\\\\\\"Could not load state dict from remote files: {e}\\\\\\\")\\\\n                print(\\\\\\\"Returning model without loading pretrained weights\\\\\\\")\\\\n                return model\\\\n\\\\n            # First, fix any key differences (see openpi model.py, _fix_pytorch_state_dict_keys)\\\\n            fixed_state_dict = model._fix_pytorch_state_dict_keys(original_state_dict, model.config)\\\\n\\\\n            # Then add \\\\\\\"model.\\\\\\\" prefix for all keys that don't already have i\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 8718 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n       images.append(img)\\\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\\\n\\\\n        return images, img_masks\\\\n\\\\n    def prepare_action(self, batch):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pad action\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        actions = pad_vector(batch[ACTION], self.config.max_action_dim)\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def select_action(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Select a single action given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        assert not self._rtc_enabled(), (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"RTC is not supported for select_action, use it with predict_action_chunk\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\\\n\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\\\n\\\\n        noise = self.model.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\\\\n        time = self.model.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\\\\n\\\\n        # Compute loss (no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        losses = self.model.forward(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, actions, noise, time)\\\\n\\\\n        # Truncate losses to actual action dimensions\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        losses = losses[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        loss_dict = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"loss_per_dim\\\\\\\": losses.mean(dim=[0, 1]).detach().cpu().numpy().tolist(),\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        if reduction == \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\":\\\\n            # Return per-sample losses (B,) by averaging over time and action dims\\\\n            per_sample_loss = losses.mean(dim=(1, 2))\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = per_sample_loss.mean().item()\\\\n            return per_sample_loss, loss_dict\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Default: return scalar mean loss\\\\n            loss = losses.mean()\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = loss.item()\\\\n            return loss, loss_dict\\\\n\\\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for PI0.5 fine-tuning.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        common_projections = (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        target_modules = rf\\\\\\\"(.*\\\\\\\\.gemma_expert\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\.self_attn\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"target_modules\\\\\\\": target_modules,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"modules_to_save\\\\\\\": [],\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"12460971-c2b7-496e-bf65-b1cba93fe402","uuid":"42af7016-a036-4e1f-bb93-7a8d839be921","timestamp":"2026-05-21T06:45:07.129Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"class PI05Policy(PreTrainedPolicy):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"PI05 Policy for LeRobot.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    config_class = PI05Config\\\\n    name = \\\\\\\"pi05\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        config: PI05Config,\\\\n        **kwargs,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            config: Policy configuration class instance.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        require_package(\\\\\\\"transformers\\\\\\\", extra=\\\\\\\"pi\\\\\\\")\\\\n        super().__init__(config)\\\\n        config.validate_features()\\\\n        self.config = config\\\\n\\\\n        # Initialize model without loading weights\\n        # Check if dataset_stats were provided in kwargs\\n        model = cls(config, **kwargs)\\n\\n        # Load state dict (expects keys with \\\"model.\\\" prefix)\\n        try:\\n            print(f\\\"Loading model from: {pretrained_name_or_path}\\\")\\n            try:\\n                from transformers.utils import cached_file\\n\\n                resolved_file = cached_file(\\n                    pretrained_name_or_path,\\n                    \\\"model.safetensors\\\",\\n                    cache_dir=kwargs.get(\\\"cache_dir\\\"),\\n                    force_download=kwargs.get(\\\"force_download\\\", False),\\n                    resume_download=kwargs.get(\\\"resume_download\\\"),\\n                    proxies=kwargs.get(\\\"proxies\\\"),\\n                    token=kwargs.get(\\\"token\\\"),\\n                    revision=kwargs.get(\\\"revision\\\"),\\n                    local_files_only=kwargs.get(\\\"local_files_only\\\", False),\\n                )\\n                from safetensors.torch import load_file\\n\\n                original_state_dict = load_file(resolved_file)\\n                print(\\\"✓ Loaded state dict from model.safetensors\\\")\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                print(f\\\"Could not load state dict from remote files: {e}\\\")\\n                print(\\\"Returning model without loading pretrained weights\\\")\\n                return model\\n\\n            # Return per-sample losses (B,) by averaging over time and action dims\\n            per_sample_loss = losses.mean(dim=(1, 2))\\n            loss_dict[\\\"loss\\\"] = per_sample_loss.mean().item()\\n            return per_sample_loss, loss_dict\\n        else:\\n            # Default: return scalar mean loss\\n            loss = losses.mean()\\n            loss_dict[\\\"loss\\\"] = loss.item()\\n            return loss, loss_dict\\n\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for PI0.5 fine-tuning.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        common_projections = (\\n            \\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\"\\n        )\\n        target_modules = rf\\\"(.*\\\\.gemma_expert\\\\..*\\\\.self_attn\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\\"target_modules\\\": target_modules,\\n            \\\"modules_to_save\\\": [],\\n        }\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.54}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01NxfFna6WSB2Ycu1zLMzN3t","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Confirmed: in transformers 5.x, `SiglipVisionModel` state dict keys do NOT have the `vision_model.` prefix. Actually, I already know the model expects keys without `vision_model.`, so I should just verify the remap logic is working correctly. The code might be right in theory—the issue could be something else entirely.\n\nLet me kill the stuck process and test directly with Pi05Wrapper by loading the model and checking the vision tower weights, though that would be slow. Maybe I should add a timeout. Actually, thinking about this differently: the earlier nohup command might have been killed before it finished. Let me try again with a cleaner setup. But the fact that loading took over 5 minutes is odd—a 3B parameter model should load in 30-60 seconds from disk, so something might be downloading in the background.\n\nNow I'm checking whether mean={p0.mean():.6f}, std={p0.std():.6f}, min={p0.min():.4f}, max={p0.max():.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n# Check if it looks like random init (Kaiming/Xavier) or pretrained\n# Random init: std ~ 0.01-0.02 for small layers, ~0.5 for large\n# Pretrained: specific trained values\np_mid = params[len(params)//2]\nprint(f\"VT param[mid] shape={p_mid.shape}, mean={p_mid.mean():.6f}, std={p_mid.std():.6f}\", flush=True)\n\n# Now apply the wrapper's _fix_vision_tower_keys and check again\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\nfrom pathlib import Path\nsd = load_file(str(Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned\") / \"model.safetensors\"))\nremap = {}\nfor k, v in sd.items():\n    if \"vision_tower.vision_model.\" in k:\n        new_k = k.replace(\"vision_tower.vision_model.\", \"vision_tower.\")\n        if not new_k.startswith(\"model.\"):\n            new_k = f\"model.{new_k}\"\n        remap[new_k] = v\n\nif remap:\n    missing, unexpected = policy.load_state_dict(remap, strict=False)\n    loaded = len(remap) - len(unexpected)\n    print(f\"_fix_vision_tower_keys: remap={len(remap)}, loaded={loaded}, unexpected={len(unexpected)}\", flush=True)\n    if unexpected:\n        print(f\"  Unexpected keys: {unexpected[:5]}\", flush=True)\n\n# Check VT weights after fix\np0_after = list(vt.parameters())[0]\nprint(f\"VT param[0] AFTER fix: mean={p0_after.mean():.6f}, std={p0_after.std():.6f}\", flush=True)\n\n# Compare\nchanged = not torch.equal(p0.data, p0_after.data)\nprint(f\"VT weights changed after _fix: {changed}\", flush=True)\n\nprint(f\"[{time.time()-t0:.1f}s] DONE\", flush=True)\nEOF\necho \"Script written\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1566,"cache_read_input_tokens":77982,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1566},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"12460971-c2b7-496e-bf65-b1cba93fe402","uuid":"558a8a45-f51b-4877-9091-2835cba45788"}

The latter starves multi-step behaviors like\\\\n    the gripper close at chunk step ~20 -- the root cause of 0% SR on PI05.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    agentview = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\\\n\\\\n    if hasattr(wrapper, \\\\\\\"predict_action\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        return wrapper.predict_action(\\\\n            agentview_rgb=agentview,\\\\n            task_name=task_name,\\\\n            robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n            eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n            K=K,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\\\n        task_name=task_name,\\\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n        K=K,\\\\n    )\\\\n    # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\\\n    k_trunc = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\\\n    mean_action = k_trunc.mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 7)\\\\n    return mean_action[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _to_scalar_uf(uf):\\\\n    return torch.stack([\\\\n        uf[:, :30].norm(dim=1),\\\\n        uf[:, 30:35].mean(dim=1),\\\\n        uf[:, 35:40].mean(dim=1),\\\\n    ], dim=1)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef select_action_crm(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs, crm, device, mode=\\\\\\\"crm_perdof\\\\\\\", obs_proj=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"ACR-VLA: CGS on 6D pose → argmin base → CRM refinement → combine with mean gripper.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    agentview = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\\\n        task_name=task_name,\\\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n        K=K,\\\\n    )\\\\n    k_full = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\\\n    k_pose = k_full[:, :, :CORRECTION_DIM]  # (K, H_eff, 6)\\\\n\\\\n    uf, _mean = cgs.compute_features(k_pose)\\\\n    uf = uf.unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\\\n    if mode == \\\\\\\"crm_scalar\\\\\\\":\\\\n        uf = _to_scalar_uf(uf)\\\\n\\\\n    # Argmin-selected base action instead of mean (avoids invalid inter-mode regions)\\\\n    k_pose_flat = k_pose.reshape(k_pose.shape[0], -1)  # (K, 30)\\\\n    mean_pose = k_pose_flat.mean(dim=0)  # (30,) reference for distance\\\\n    distances = ((k_pose_flat - mean_pose) ** 2).sum(dim=-1)  # (K,)\\\\n    best_idx = distances.argmin()\\\\n    base_action = k_pose_flat[best_idx].unsqueeze(0).to(device)  # (1, 30)\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat = obs_feat.to(device)\\\\n    if obs_proj is not None:\\\\n        obs_feat = obs_proj(obs_feat)\\\\n\\\\n    uf_input = None if mode == \\\\\\\"crm_obs_only\\\\\\\" else uf\\\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\\\n        refined_pose = crm(base_action, uf_input, obs_feat)  # (1, 30)\\\\n\\\\n    refined_pose = refined_pose[0].reshape(H_EFF, CORRECTION_DIM)\\\\n    mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 1)\\\\n    refined_full = torch.cat([refined_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)  # (H_eff, 7)\\\\n\\\\n    return refined_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef select_action_reconvla(wrapper, obs, task_name, K):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"ReconVLA baseline: scalar Euclidean argmin on 6D pose, pass-through gripper.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    agentview = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\\\n        task_name=task_name,\\\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n        K=K,\\\\n    )\\\\n    k_full = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\\\n    k_pose = k_full[:, :, :CORRECTION_DIM]  # (K, H_eff, 6)\\\\n    selected_pose = reconvla_select(k_pose)  # (H_eff, 6)\\\\n    mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 1)\\\\n    selected_full = torch.cat([selected_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\\\n    return selected_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nd\\\\n\\\\n... {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01GAXeiqpu36CGc7jvmMKjfe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"`compile_model: true` with `compile_mode: \"max-autotune\"`! This is very expensive. `torch.compile` with `max-autotune` compiles the model code to optimized kernels, and the compilation itself can take 5-15 minutes. This explains the long wait.\n\nBut this isn't the real issue here. The `torch.compile` only compiles during the first forward pass, not during model construction. The actual `torch.compile` call just wraps the function, it doesn't compile immediately. Compilation happens lazily when the compiled function is first called.\n \nThe real bottleneck is probably the `.to(config.device)` call that moves the ~3B parameters from CPU to GPU. init_samples[:, 1, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n            per_step_l2 = diff.norm(dim=-1)\\\\n            mean_l2 = per_step_l2.mean(dim=-1)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            dists = []\\\\n            for i in range(k_init):\\\\n                for j in range(i + 1, k_init):\\\\n                    diff = init_samples[:, i, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM] - init_samples[:, j, :, :FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\\\n                    dists.append(diff.norm(dim=-1).mean(dim=-1))\\\\n            mean_l2 = torch.stack(dists).mean(dim=0)\\\\n\\\\n        needs_full = (mean_l2 >= threshold).any().item()\\\\n        l2_val = mean_l2.item() if B == 1 else mean_l2.cpu().tolist()\\\\n\\\\n        if not needs_full:\\\\n            return init_samples, {\\\\n                'path': 'fast', 'actual_k': k_init, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n            }\\\\n\\\\n        extra_samples = self.generate_k_samples(\\\\n            past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks, K=k_full - k_init\\\\n        )\\\\n        all_samples = torch.cat([init_samples, extra_samples], dim=1)\\\\n        return all_samples, {\\\\n            'path': 'full', 'actual_k': k_full, 'l2_distance': l2_val,\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples_adaptive(self, agentview_rgb, task_name,\\\\n                                               robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb=None,\\\\n                                               threshold=0.1, k_init=2, k_full=5):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"End-to-end adaptive K: obs → features + adaptive samples + metadata.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, robot_state, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples, metadata = self.generate_adaptive_k_samples(\\\\n            kv_cache, pad_masks, threshold, k_init, k_full\\\\n        )\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples, metadata\\\\n\\\\n    def reset_action_queue(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Clear cached chunk. chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM) -- average over K\\\\n            chunk = samples[0].mean(dim=0)\\\\n            chunk_np = chunk.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n            for i in {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Se22DGSPHc2XoYBQg6BA4s","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def _preprocess_images(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> tuple[list[Tensor], list[Tensor]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Preprocess images for the model.\\\\n\\\\n        Images from LeRobot are typically in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [0, 1].\\\\n        PaliGemma expects images in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [-1, 1].\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        images = []\\\\n        img_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Get device from model parameters\\\\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\\\\n\\\\n        present_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key in batch]\\\\n        missing_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key not in batch]\\\\n\\\\n        if len(present_img_keys) == 0:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"All image features are missing from the batch. At least one expected. \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"(batch: {batch.keys()}) (image_features: {self.config.image_features})\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n        # Preprocess image features present in the batch\\\\n        for key in present_img_keys:\\\\n            img = batch[key]\\\\n\\\\n            # Ensure tensor is on the same device as the model\\\\n            if img.device != device:\\\\n                img = img.to(device)\\\\n\\\\n            # Ensure float32 dtype for consistency\\\\n            if img.dtype != torch.float32:\\\\n                img = img.to(torch.float32)\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Handle both [B, C, H, W] and [B, H, W, C] formats\\\\n            is_channels_first = img.shape[1] == 3  # Check if channels are in dimension 1\\\\n\\\\n            if is_channels_first:\\\\n                # Convert [B, C, H, W] to [B, H, W, C] for processing\\\\n                img = img.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Resize with padding if needed\\\\n            if img.shape[1:3] != self.config.image_resolution:\\\\n                img = resize_with_pad_torch(img, *self.config.image_resolution)\\\\n\\\\n            # Normalize from [0,1] to [-1,1] as expected by siglip\\\\n            img = img * 2.0 - 1.0\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Convert back to [B, C, H, W] format if it was originally channels-first\\\\n            if is_channels_first:\\\\n                img = img.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)  # [B, H, W, C] -> [B, C, H, W]\\\\n\\\\n            images.append(img)\\\\n            # Create mask (all ones for real images)\\\\n            bsize = img.shape[0]\\\\n            mask = torch.ones(bsize, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\\\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\\\n\\\\n        # Create image features not present in the batch as fully 0 padded images\\\\n        for _num_empty_cameras in range(len(missing_img_keys)):\\\\n            img = torch.ones_like(img) * -1  # Padded with -1 for SigLIP\\\\n            mask = torch.zeros_like(mask)  # Mask is zero for empty cameras\\\\n            images.append(img)\\\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\\\n\\\\n        return images, img_masks\\\\n\\\\n    def prepare_action(self, batch):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pad action\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        actions = pad_vector(batch[ACTION], self.config.max_action_dim)\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def select_action(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Select a single action given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        assert not self._rtc_enabled(), (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"RTC is not supported for select_action, use it with predict_action_chunk\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\\\n\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\\\n\\\\n        noise = self.model.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\\\\n        time = self.model.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\\\\n\\\\n        # Compute loss (no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        losses = self.model.forward(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, actions, noise, time)\\\\n\\\\n        # Truncate losses to actual action dimensions\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        losses = losses[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        loss_dict = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"loss_per_dim\\\\\\\": losses.mean(dim=[0, 1]).detach().cpu().numpy().tolist(),\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        if reduction == \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\":\\\\n            # Return per-sample losses (B,) by averaging over time and action dims\\\\n            per_sample_loss = losses.mean(dim=(1, 2))\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = per_sample_loss.mean().item()\\\\n            return per_sample_loss, loss_dict\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Default: return scalar mean loss\\\\n            loss = losses.mean()\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = loss.item()\\\\n            return loss, loss_dict\\\\n\\\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for PI0.5 fine-tuning.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        common_projections = (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        target_modules = rf\\\\\\\"(.*\\\\\\\\.gemma_expert\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\.self_attn\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"target_modules\\\\\\\": target_modules,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"modules_to_save\\\\\\\": [],\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"12460971-c2b7-496e-bf65-b1cba93fe402","uuid":"fabd83d7-d881-42d2-8e56-e56dd6c86e9f","timestamp":"2026-05-21T06:53:04.922Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def _preprocess_images(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> tuple[list[Tensor], list[Tensor]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Preprocess images for the model.\\\\n\\\\n        Images from LeRobot are typically in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [0, 1].\\\\n        PaliGemma expects images in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [-1, 1].\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        images = []\\\\n        img_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\\\n\\\\n        noise = self.model.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\\\\n        time = self.model.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\\\\n\\\\n        # Compute loss (no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        losses = self.model.forward(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, actions, noise, time)\\\\n\\\\n        # Truncate losses to actual action dimensions\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        losses = losses[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        loss_dict = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"loss_per_dim\\\\\\\": losses.mean(dim=[0, 1]).detach().cpu().numpy().tolist(),\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        if reduction == \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\":\\\\n            # Return per-sample losses (B,) by averaging over time and action dims\\\\n            per_sample_loss = losses.mean(dim=(1, 2))\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = per_sample_loss.mean().item()\\\\n            return per_sample_loss, loss_dict\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Default: return scalar mean loss\\\\n            loss = losses.mean()\\\\n            loss_dict[\\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\"] = loss.item()\\\\n            return loss, loss_dict\\\\n\\\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for PI0.5 fine-tuning.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        common_projections = (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        target_modules = rf\\\\\\\"(.*\\\\\\\\.gemma_expert\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\.self_attn\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"target_modules\\\\\\\": target_modules,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"modules_to_save\\\\\\\": [],\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.36}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"    def _preprocess_images(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> tuple[list[Tensor], list[Tensor]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Preprocess images for the model.\\n\\n        Images from LeRobot are typically in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [0, 1].\\n        PaliGemma expects images in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [-1, 1].\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        images = []\\n        img_masks = []\\n\\n        # Get device from model parameters\\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\\n\\n        present_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key in batch]\\n        missing_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key not in batch]\\n\\n        if len(present_img_keys) == 0:\\n            raise ValueError(\\n                f\\\"All image features are missing from the batch. At least one expected. \\\"\\n                f\\\"(batch: {batch.keys()}) (image_features: {self.config.image_features})\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # Preprocess image features present in the batch\\n        for key in present_img_keys:\\n            img = batch[key]\\n\\n            # Ensure tensor is on the same device as the model\\n            if img.device != device:\\n                img = img.to(device)\\n\\n            # Ensure float32 dtype for consistency\\n            if img.dtype != torch.float32:\\n                img = img.to(torch.float32)\\n\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Handle both [B, C, H, W] and [B, H, W, C] formats\\n            is_channels_first = img.shape[1] == 3  # Check if channels are in dimension 1\\n\\n            if is_channels_first:\\n                # Convert [B, C, H, W] to [B, H, W, C] for processing\\n                img = img.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)\\n\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Resize with padding if needed\\n            if img.shape[1:3] != self.config.image_resolution:\\n                img = resize_with_pad_torch(img, *self.config.image_resolution)\\n\\n            # Normalize from [0,1] to [-1,1] as expected by siglip\\n            img = img * 2.0 - 1.0\\n\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Convert back to [B, C, H, W] format if it was originally channels-first\\n            if is_channels_first:\\n                img = img.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)  # [B, H, W, C] -> [B, C, H, W]\\n\\n            images.append(img)\\n            # Create mask (all ones for real images)\\n            bsize = img.shape[0]\\n            mask = torch.ones(bsize, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\n\\n        # Create image features not present in the batch as fully 0 padded images\\n        for _num_empty_cameras in range(len(missing_img_keys)):\\n            img = torch.ones_like(img) * -1  # Padded with -1 for SigLIP\\n            mask = torch.zeros_like(mask)  # Mask is zero for empty cameras\\n            images.append(img)\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\n\\n        return images, img_masks\\n\\n    def prepare_action(self, batch):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Pad action\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions = pad_vector(batch[ACTION], self.config.max_action_dim)\\n        return actions\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def select_action(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select a single action given environment observations.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        assert not self._rtc_enabled(), (\\n            \\\"RTC is not supported for select_action, use it with predict_action_chunk\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        self.eval()\\n\\n        # Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\n\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.eval()\\n\\n        # Prepare inputs\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\"], batch[f\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\"]\\n\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\n\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\n\\n        return actions\\n\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\"mean\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\n                - \\\"mean\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\n                - \\\"none\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Prepare inputs\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\"], batch[f\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\"]\\n\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\n\\n        noise = self.model.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\\n        time = self.model.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\\n\\n        # Compute loss (no separate state needed for PI05)\\n        losses = self.model.forward(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, actions, noise, time)\\n\\n        # Truncate losses to actual action dimensions\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\n        losses = losses[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\n\\n        loss_dict = {\\n            \\\"loss_per_dim\\\": losses.mean(dim=[0, 1]).detach().cpu().numpy().tolist(),\\n        }\\n\\n        if reduction == \\\"none\\\":\\n            # Return per-sample losses (B,) by averaging over time and action dims\\n            per_sample_loss = losses.mean(dim=(1, 2))\\n            loss_dict[\\\"loss\\\"] = per_sample_loss.mean().item()\\n            return per_sample_loss, loss_dict\\n        else:\\n            # Default: return scalar mean loss\\n            loss = losses.mean()\\n            loss_dict[\\\"loss\\\"] = loss.item()\\n            return loss, loss_dict\\n\\n    def _get_default_peft_targets(self) -> dict[str, any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return default PEFT target modules for PI0.5 fine-tuning.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        common_projections = (\\n            \\\"state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out\\\"\\n        )\\n        target_modules = rf\\\"(.*\\\\.gemma_expert\\\\..*\\\\.self_attn\\\\.(q|v)_proj|model\\\\.({common_projections}))\\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\\"target_modules\\\": target_modules,\\n            \\\"modules_to_save\\\": [],\\n        }\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MRgHCWfYZ3pafrb8cxEDiU","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"def resize_with_pad_torch(  # see openpi `resize_with_pad_torch` (exact copy)\\\\n    images: torch.Tensor,\\\\n    height: int,\\\\n    width: int,\\\\n    mode: str = \\\\\\\"bilinear\\\\\\\",\\\\n) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"PyTorch version of resize_with_pad. Resizes an image to a target height and width without distortion\\\\n    by padding with black. If the image is float32, it must be in the range [-1, 1].\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        images: Tensor of shape [*b, h, w, c] or [*b, c, h, w]\\\\n        height: Target height\\\\n        width: Target width\\\\n        mode: Interpolation mode ('bilinear', 'nearest', etc.)\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Resized and padded tensor with same shape format as input\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # Check if input is in channels-last format [*b, h, w, c] or channels-first [*b, c, h, w]\\\\n    if images.shape[-1] <= 4:  # Assume channels-last format\\\\n        channels_last = True\\\\n        if images.dim() == 3:\\\\n            images = images.unsqueeze(0)  # Add batch dimension\\\\n        images = images.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)  # [b, h, w, c] -> [b, c, h, w]\\\\n    else:\\\\n        channels_last = False\\\\n        if images.dim() == 3:\\\\n            images = images.unsqueeze(0)  # Add batch dimension\\\\n\\\\n    batch_size, channels, cur_height, cur_width = images.shape\\\\n\\\\n    # Calculate resize ratio\\\\n    ratio = max(cur_width / width, cur_height / height)\\\\n    resized_height = int(cur_height / ratio)\\\\n    resized_width = int(cur_width / ratio)\\\\n\\\\n    # Resize\\\\n    resized_images = F.interpolate(\\\\n        images,\\\\n        size=(resized_height, resized_width),\\\\n        mode=mode,\\\\n        align_corners=False if mode == \\\\\\\"bilinear\\\\\\\" else None,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    # Handle dtype-specific clipping\\\\n    if images.dtype == torch.uint8:\\\\n        resized_images = torch.round(resized_images).clamp(0, 255).to(torch.uint8)\\\\n    elif images.dtype == torch.float32:\\\\n        resized_images = resized_images.clamp(0.0, 1.0)\\\\n    else:\\\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Unsupported image dtype: {images.dtype}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Calculate padding\\\\n    pad_h0, remainder_h = divmod(height - resized_height, 2)\\\\n    pad_h1 = pad_h0 + remainder_h\\\\n    pad_w0, remainder_w = divmod(width - resized_width, 2)\\\\n    pad_w1 = pad_w0 + remainder_w\\\\n\\\\n    # Pad\\\\n    constant_value = 0 if images.dtype == torch.uint8 else 0.0\\\\n    padded_images = F.pad(\\\\n        resized_images,\\\\n        (pad_w0, pad_w1, pad_h0, pad_h1),  # left, right, top, bottom\\\\n        mode=\\\\\\\"constant\\\\\\\",\\\\n        value=constant_value,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    #
